  //
// CustomEvent -- polyfill for IE11
//             -- adapted from Mozilla's: https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/CustomEvent/CustomEvent#Polyfill

if (typeof window.CustomEvent !== 'function') {
  var default_event_params = {
    bubbles: false,
    cancelable: false,
    detail: undefined,
  };

  var CustomEvent = function(event_type, params) {
    params = params || default_event_params;
    var ev = document.createEvent('CustomEvent');
    ev.initCustomEvent(event_type, params.bubbles, params.cancelable, params.detail);
    return ev;
  }

  // Touch device detection
  // (https://stackoverflow.com/questions/4817029/whats-the-best-way-to-detect-a-touch-screen-device-using-javascript/4819886#4819886)
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------

  window.isTouchDevice= function() {
    var prefixes = ' -webkit- -moz- -o- -ms- '.split(' ');
    var mq = function(query) {
      return window.matchMedia(query).matches;
    }
    if (('ontouchstart' in window) || window.DocumentTouch && document instanceof DocumentTouch) {
      return true;
    }
    // include the 'heartz' as a way to have a non matching MQ to help terminate the join
    // https://git.io/vznFH
    var query = ['(', prefixes.join('touch-enabled),('), 'heartz', ')'].join('');
    return mq(query);
  }
  if (window.isTouchDevice()) {
    document.documentElement.className += ' is-touch';
  }


  // Add class to body if Internet Explorer
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------

  if (!!navigator.userAgent.match(/Trident/g) || !!navigator.userAgent.match(/MSIE/g)) {
      document.documentElement.className += ' is-ie';
  }


  // Page load transitions
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------

  function apply_page_load_classes() {
    var items = document.querySelectorAll('[data-t-page-load-wrapper]');
    for (i = 0; i < items.length; ++i) {
      var active_class = items[i].getAttribute('data-t-page-load-wrapper-active-class');
      items[i].classList.add(active_class);
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', apply_page_load_classes);

// Drift playbook AB testing: see https://bit.ly/2Ky3BTq
// ----------------------------------------

(function() {
  function getRandomInt(min, max) {
    min = Math.ceil(min);
    max = Math.floor(max);
    return Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min)) + min;
  }
  var random = getRandomInt(1, 100);

  var pb = 'D';
  if (random <= 25) { pb = 'A'; }
  else if (random <= 50) { pb = 'B'; }
  else if (random <= 75) { pb = 'C'; }

  document.cookie = 'DriftPlaybook=' + pb;
})();
